- **memory-systems-zh**: Agent 记忆系统设计、跨会话知识保留、长期记忆、实体追踪与身份一致、时间有效性（事实会过期）、向量检索 vs 知识图谱 vs 时态知识图谱、记忆整理与合并、记忆框架选型（Mem0 / Zep-Graphiti / Letta / LangMem / Cognee / 文件系统）、记忆基准（LoCoMo / LongMemEval / DMR）。当用户说「Agent 怎么记住上次会话的事」「用什么记忆框架」「同一个人在不同对话里怎么保持一致」「过期的记忆污染了回答」「要不要上知识图谱」时使用。核心：记忆是从易失的上下文窗口到持久存储的光谱，默认选满足检索需求的最浅一层（先文件系统原型，再向量+元数据，再时态图谱，最后自管理记忆）；检索策略按查询形状匹配（语义 / 实体遍历 / 时间过滤 / 混合）；周期性整理——失效但不删除；记忆按需即时加载并放在注意力偏好位置；为检索失败设计兜底。附实现参考（向量库、属性图、时态图、整理器）。改编自 Agent Skills for Context Engineering 的 memory-systems（MIT）。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/ported/skills/memory-systems-zh) · ★4)

- **memv-files**: This skill should be used when the user asks to 'upload a PDF to memv', 'ingest a document', 'add an image to memv', 'process audio with memv', or when calling client.upload.batch.create. Covers multimodal-first ingestion and when to use upload.batch.create vs memories.add. For video specifically, use memv-video-ingest instead. ([drPod/memv-skills](https://github.com/drPod/memv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/memv-files))
- **memv-graph**: This skill should be used when the user asks to 'query the memv knowledge graph', 'inspect entities and relationships', 'get triplets for a memory', 'find connections between', or when calling client.graph.retrieve_triplets. Covers entity/relationship extraction, when to use explicit graph queries vs the graph-aware client.memories.search, and the per-memory triplet model. ([drPod/memv-skills](https://github.com/drPod/memv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/memv-graph))
- **memv-mcp-setup**: This skill should be used when the user asks to 'install memv MCP', 'wire memv to Claude Code/Cursor/ChatGPT/Claude Desktop', 'add MCP server', 'expose our app via MCP', or 'build an MCP server'. Covers per-client install commands, OAuth flow, endpoint conventions, and the limits of the 4-tool surface. ([drPod/memv-skills](https://github.com/drPod/memv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/memv-mcp-setup))
- **memv-mcp-vs-sdk**: This skill should be used when the user is deciding between mcp__memv__* tools and SDK code, asks 'should I use MCP or SDK', mixes mcp__memv__ calls with memvai imports in app code, or hits the workspaceId/space_id terminology mismatch. Covers the agent-vs-app boundary and the 4-tool MCP surface limits. ([drPod/memv-skills](https://github.com/drPod/memv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/memv-mcp-vs-sdk))
- **memv-search**: This skill should be used when the user asks to 'search memv memories', 'retrieve from the knowledge graph', 'find memories about X', 'implement semantic search on memv', or when calling client.memories.search. Covers graph-aware ranking, when retrieval will surprise you, and the difference from explicit graph traversal. ([drPod/memv-skills](https://github.com/drPod/memv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/memv-search))
- **memv-spaces**: This skill should be used when the user asks to 'create a memv space', 'list workspaces', 'delete a space', 'design memv tenancy', 'isolate memories per user', or when calling client.spaces.* methods. Covers per-user / per-feature / per-environment partitioning patterns and the no-global-write rule. ([drPod/memv-skills](https://github.com/drPod/memv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/memv-spaces))
- **memv-video-ingest**: This skill should be used when the user asks to 'upload a video to memv', 'ingest video', 'extract memories from video', 'process a video file', or when calling client.upload.batch.create with a video file. Covers multimodal extraction (audio + visual + on-screen text + temporal dynamics), upload size considerations, and why pre-transcribing loses information. ([drPod/memv-skills](https://github.com/drPod/memv-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/memv-video-ingest))
- **memvault**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/context-management/memvault) · ★745)
- **memvault**: Production-ready long-term memory server for AI agents with Ebbinghaus decay and strength-weighted retrieval. Use when you need persistent memory across agent sessions, memory decay (forgetting curve), memory statistics, or multi-agent memory tracking. Triggers: long-term memory, remember, recall, memory decay, Ebbinghaus, agent memory, memvault. Requires Docker. ([Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge/tree/HEAD/community/clawhub/m/memvault) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **memvault**: Give the agent long-term memory that survives across sessions, using the MemVault MCP server (local, no cloud). Use when the user wants the agent to remember facts, preferences, or decisions between conversations, to recall what it knew before, or to forget something on request. Covers installing MemVault, connecting it as an MCP server, and the basic write/search/forget loop. ([swap-mitra/memvault](https://github.com/swap-mitra/memvault/tree/HEAD/skills/memvault))
- **memvault-recall**: Search the user's shared MemVault memory store. Use when the user references past conversations ("I told you before...", "as usual...", "remember...") or when personal/project context would change the answer and current context has none. ([dreamor/MemVault](https://github.com/dreamor/MemVault/tree/HEAD/.openclaw/skills/memvault-recall) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **memvault-review**: Work the MemVault review inbox — inspect auto-extracted memory drafts (with their source snippets) and approve, edit, or reject each one. Use when the user asks to review memories, or /memvault-review is invoked. ([dreamor/MemVault](https://github.com/dreamor/MemVault/tree/HEAD/.openclaw/skills/memvault-review) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **memvault-save**: Write a durable learning (explicit user preference, correction, project decision) into the shared MemVault store so every future session and agent inherits it. Use whenever the user states a lasting preference or corrects course, or asks to remember something. ([dreamor/MemVault](https://github.com/dreamor/MemVault/tree/HEAD/.openclaw/skills/memvault-save) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **memvault-sync**: Regenerate agent-native rule files (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, .github/copilot-instructions.md) from the MemVault store for hosts without hook injection. Use when the user asks to sync/refresh memory into rule files, or /memvault-sync is invoked. ([dreamor/MemVault](https://github.com/dreamor/MemVault/tree/HEAD/.openclaw/skills/memvault-sync) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **memvid**: Single-file AI memory with memvid-cli for persistent knowledge storage and retrieval using text-embedding-3-large (3072 dimensions). Use when storing learnings, searching past context, managing session memory, or implementing long-term memory for AI agents. Triggers include "remember", "recall", "search memory", "store this", "what did we learn", or any task requiring persistent memory across sessions. ([DxTa/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/DxTa/claude-code-skills/tree/HEAD/data/memvid))
- **memvid**: Memory layer for AI Agents. Replace complex RAG pipelines with a serverless, single-file memory layer. Give your agents instant retrieval and long-term memory. ([Feahter/hermes-skills](https://github.com/Feahter/hermes-skills/tree/HEAD/note-taking/memvid))
- **memvid-backup**: Usar cuando se crea un backup portable de la memoria externa de Savia. ([gonzalezpazmonica/savia](https://github.com/gonzalezpazmonica/savia/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/memvid-backup) · ★49)
- **memvid-rag**: Local RAG over PDF documents using memvid. Use when the user wants to index documents into a local knowledge base, semantically search previously indexed documents, or check the status of the knowledge base. ([fa-ina-tic/memvid-rag](https://github.com/fa-ina-tic/memvid-rag/tree/HEAD/skills/memvid-rag) · ★3)
- **memwal**: Walrus Memory SDK — portable agent memory that works across apps, sessions, and workflows. Use when users say: - "add memory to my app" - "portable agent memory" - "integrate Walrus Memory" - "AI agent memory" - "memory across agents" - "Walrus memory storage" - "setup Walrus Memory" - "recall memories" ([MystenLabs/walrus-memory](https://github.com/MystenLabs/walrus-memory/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★136 · 2 copies)
- **memx**: Use when an agent needs to recall, save, inspect, or audit memX memories through the bundled MCP tools. ([NeoLi00/memX](https://github.com/NeoLi00/memX/tree/HEAD/skills/memx) · ★401)

Įjungti, kai vartotojas prašo „kalbėk juokingai / memais / kaip zoomeris“, „įjunk memų režimą“, „papasakok juokelį“, arba kai pokalbis akivaizdžiai neformalus.
